    // Gets entire cookie
    // Looks for a cookie with the name passed into cookieName
    // Gets the value for cookie with the cookieName
    function getCookie(cookieName) {
      var name = cookieName + "=";
      var decoded = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
      var cookies = decoded.split(';');
      for(var i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
        var cookie = cookies[i];
        while (cookie.charAt(0) == ' ') {
          cookie = cookie.substring(1);
        }
        if (cookie.indexOf(name) == 0) {
          return cookie.substring(name.length, cookie.length);
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    // Gets cookie to redirect to appropriate page
    var redirectToPage = getCookie('redirectToPage');

    // Redirects to page if user has previously made a choice
    if (redirectToPage && location.search != '?select-a-region') {
      window.location.replace(redirectToPage);
    }
  </script>

<script>
  document.documentElement.className = document.documentElement.className.replace("no-js", "js");
</script>
<script>
  $(function () {
    $('.landing-page-accordion-content > div').removeClass("row"); //.css('float', 'left').css('width', '100%').css('border-bottom', 'none');
    $('.landing-page-accordion').not(':first').css('border-top', '1px solid #FF6900');
    $('.landing-page-accordion').on('click', function () {
      // click
      var content = $(this).next();
      var show = content.css('display') != 'block';
      $('.landing-page-accordion').removeClass('accord_enter').css('border-bottom', 'none').next().hide();
      $('.landing-page-accordion i').removeClass('fa-minus').addClass('fa-plus');
      $('svg g').removeClass('region_select region_enter');
      if (show) {
        $(this).css('border-bottom', '1px solid #FF6900').addClass('accord_enter').find('i').removeClass('fa-plus').addClass("fa-minus");
        //content.css('border-bottom', '1px solid #FF6900');
        content.show();
        var reg = $(this).data('region');
        $('#' + reg).addClass('region_select');
      }

    }).hover(function () {
      // mouse enter
      var content = $(this).next();
      var show = content.css('display') != 'block';
      if (show) {
        var reg = $(this).data('region');
        $('#' + reg).addClass('region_enter');
        $(this).addClass('accord_enter');
      }
    }, function () {
      // mouse leave
      var content = $(this).next();
      var show = content.css('display') != 'block';
      if (show) {
        var reg = $(this).data('region');
        $('#' + reg).removeClass('region_enter');
        $(this).removeClass('accord_enter');
      }
    });
    // world map
    $('svg g').on('click', function () {
      var reg = $(this).attr('id');
      $('div[data-region="' + reg + '"]').click();
    }).hover(function () {
      // mouse enter
      var reg = $(this).attr('id');
      var show = !$(this).hasClass('region_select');
      if (show) {
        $('div[data-region="' + reg + '"]').addClass('accord_enter');
      }
      $(this).addClass('region_enter');
    }, function () {
      // mouse leave
      var reg = $(this).attr('id');
      var show = !$(this).hasClass('region_select');
      if (show) {
        $('div[data-region="' + reg + '"]').removeClass('accord_enter');
      }
      $(this).removeClass('region_enter');
    });
    //$('div[data-region="america"]').click();
  });

  /*
  var accordionHeaders = document.getElementsByClassName("landing-page-accordion");
  var accordionContents = document.getElementsByClassName("landing-page-accordion-content");

  for (var i = 0; i < accordionHeaders.length; i++) {
    accordionHeaders[i].addEventListener("click", function(){
      var content = this.nextElementSibling;
    for (var j = 0; j < accordionContents.length; j++) {
      if (accordionContents[j] !== content || content.style.display == "block") {
          accordionContents[j].style.display = "none";
      accordionHeaders[j].childNodes[3].firstChild.classList.add("fa-plus");
      accordionHeaders[j].childNodes[3].firstChild.classList.remove("fa-minus");
    }
        else {
        content.style.display = "block";
      accordionHeaders[j].childNodes[3].firstChild.classList.add("fa-minus");
      accordionHeaders[j].childNodes[3].firstChild.classList.remove("fa-plus");
    }
      }
    });
  }
  */
</script>
